- **caveman-compress**: &gt; **Data leaves the machine.** Compression sends the file's full text to the model provider that `scripts/compress.py` is configured for: the `claude` CLI, the Anthropic API, or an OpenAI-compatible endpoint. Files whose names look like secrets (`.env`, `credentials`, `secrets`, `.netrc`, …) are refused before they are read. Don't compress a file the provider shouldn't see. - **cbt**: Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (Aaron Beck) - Identify and restructure unhelpful thinking patterns through the thoughts-feelings-behaviors triangle ([ironyjk/counsel-frameworks](https://github.com/ironyjk/counsel-frameworks/tree/HEAD/cbt) · ★4)
- **cbt**: Sourced, adversarially verified research corpus on TCC (thérapie cognitivo-comportementale / cognitive behavioural therapy), compiled 2026-07-28 from ~210 cited primary sources. Use for any question about CBT or TCC — what it is, whether it works for a given disorder and how well, effect sizes and why headline numbers are inflated, techniques and session doses, exposure and ERP, behavioural activation, third-wave therapies (ACT, DBT, MBCT, schema therapy, MCT), EMDR versus trauma-focused CBT, the guideline landscape (NICE, HAS, APA, WHO mhGAP, VA/DoD), internet CBT, mental-health apps and AI… ([Ephasme/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ephasme/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/health/cbt))
- **CBT-based Self-Acceptance and Perspective Shift Protocol**: A structured CBT-informed macro-protocol for clients with interpersonal difficulties rooted in negative self-perception; focuses on building self-acceptance as prerequisite to relational change, then systematically shifting cognitive perspective toward positivity and strengths. ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT-based%20Self-Acceptance%20and%20Perspective%20Shift%20Protocol) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **cbt-bench-eval**: Evaluates LLMs on cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT) assistance across basic knowledge recall and cognitive model understanding. The benchmark probes multiple-choice knowledge acquisition and multi-label classification of cognitive distortions and core beliefs to measure therapeutic applicability and fine-grained clinical reasoning. Use when the user wants to benchmark on CBT-QA, CBT-CD, CBT-PC, CBT-FC, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Accuracy, F1. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/cbt-bench-eval) · ★3)
- **cbt-chaos-analyst-v2**: Independently validate one CBT chaos attempt from raw injection evidence and deterministic lifecycle checks, investigate unexpected CR/log behavior, and write analysis.json. Draft evidence-backed per-defect bugs and proposed new scenario Jira issues without executing them. Spawned by cbt-chaos-lead-v2; never authors or executes the approved chaos bundle and never reruns E2E. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-analyst-v2))
- **cbt-chaos-evidence-checker**: Blind CBT chaos team checker that grades whether every scenario acceptance criterion has sufficient run-scoped evidence from the correct run, object UIDs, and time intervals — and actively hunts false-positive paths such as Done=True masking failure, .status.type disagrees with physical qcow2 type, or checkpoint-name fields without chain proof. Returns PASS or REJECT; a REJECT forces BLOCKED/UNKNOWN, never a downgraded PASS. Used when the Lead spawns the Evidence Sufficiency Checker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/check-evidence.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-evidence-checker))
- **cbt-chaos-flow-analyst**: Post-run CBT chaos team agent that hunts weaknesses in the CBT product flow itself — the thing under test: silent corruption, false Incremental, checkpoint/tracker inconsistency, stuck finalizers, recovery failures, loud-vs-silent failure semantics. Drafts engineering tickets per the template at the scenario level. Proposes findings only; never mutates. Used when the Lead spawns the CBT Flow Analyst after the run reaches closed. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-flow-analyst))
- **cbt-chaos-injection-checker**: Blind CBT chaos team checker that grades only injection validity: was the chaos action executed inside the scenario's specified window, on the exact target (kind/name/UID/owner, correct cardinality), with an independently observed effect? Returns PASS or REJECT; a REJECT forces the run verdict to BLOCKED. Receives only run-scoped evidence and the spec — never worker narratives or the Lead's conclusions. Used when the Lead spawns the Injection Validity Checker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/check-injection.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-injection-checker))
- **cbt-chaos-issue-triage**: Triage a CBT Chaos v2 test by reading its Jira issue under cbt-chaos/scenarios_v2/&lt;scenario_id&gt;/jira-issue.md, checking the actual cluster and the active virt-cbt-lab workflow, validating the chaos injection against the live Krkn registry, and generating a detailed scenario-spec.md plus a minimal chaos-trigger.sh. Use for designing or preparing a v2 scenario; do not execute E2E or inject chaos unless separately requested. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-issue-triage))
- **cbt-chaos-krkn-preparer**: CBT chaos team design-time specialist that turns a disruption intent into a validated krknctl command: loads the krkn-scenario skill for flag accuracy, queries live krknctl metadata, builds the command, validates event-trigger feasibility for the injection window, dry-runs the exact argv, and returns compact evidence — or a justified scoped oc/kubectl fallback when Krkn cannot express the action. Used at triage (new scenarios) and improvement (trigger corrections); never mid-run, never executes the disruption live. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-krkn-preparer))
- **cbt-chaos-lead-v2**: Own one CBT chaos scenario end to end as the dedicated Lead subagent: prepare through specialists, relay human approval through main, execute the approved bundle, obtain independent grading, report and verify local closeout. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-lead-v2))
- **cbt-chaos-observer-worker**: Long-lived read-only CBT chaos team worker and the run's independent injection witness. Arms scoped Kubernetes watches (ARMED), preserves terminal evidence before recovery (PRESERVED), delivers a bounded final analysis (FINAL), and keeps raw streams on disk. Exactly one per run; never mutates, never grades. Used when the Lead spawns the Observer Worker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/observer.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-observer-worker))
- **cbt-chaos-preflight-worker**: Short-lived CBT chaos team worker that validates one scenario run before any live execution: spec/trigger/cluster agreement, trigger script static checks, Krkn dry-run, cluster readiness, and destination PVC capacity headroom. Returns GO or NO-GO only; never mutates anything. Used when the Lead spawns the Preflight Worker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/preflight.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-preflight-worker))
- **cbt-chaos-recovery-worker**: Short-lived CBT chaos team worker that runs the post-chaos same-VM recovery validation: new full/incremental backup on the recovered original VM with fresh object/PVC names, tracker/checkpoint checks, and restore verification. Acts only on Lead authorization per action, after PRESERVED. Used when the Lead spawns the Recovery Worker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/recovery.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-recovery-worker))
- **cbt-chaos-report-checker**: Blind CBT chaos team checker that grades report fidelity: the two run reports must be standalone-readable, contain only observed facts, cite real evidence, and contain no harness internals, reconstructed commands, or expectations stated as facts. Returns PASS or REJECT (revision loop). Used when the Lead spawns the Report Fidelity Checker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/check-report.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-report-checker))
